Initial Approval Notification
    ->
Privacy Governance Case
    ->
Reminder Scheduler
    ->
Reminder Email Service
    ->
Reminder Email Sender
    ->
Email Provider
    ->
Resend

The inspection specifically checks preservation of:

- Change ID
- Case ID
- Initial notification relationship
- Reminder relationship
- Case URL
- Notification type
- Reminder reason
- Governance status
- Audit/correlation identifiers
- Email subject/body
- Provider boundary

1. PRIVACY APPROVAL EMAIL SERVICE

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from .privacy_change import PrivacyChange


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ApprovalEmailRequest:
    """Immutable request to escalate a privacy change for human approval."""

    change_id: str
    recipient: str
    subject: str
    body: str
    requires_dpo_approval: bool
    requires_grc_approval: bool


class ApprovalEmailSender(Protocol):
    """Application-level email delivery boundary.

    The application layer does not know whether delivery uses
    Resend, SMTP, or another provider.
    """

    def send(
        self,
        request: ApprovalEmailRequest,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PrivacyApprovalEmailService:
    """Creates privacy governance approval requests.

    Responsibilities:
    - validate that automated privacy validation has passed;
    - determine whether DPO/GRC review is required;
    - construct the approval request;
    - delegate email delivery to the injected sender.

    Does NOT:
    - approve privacy changes;
    - make legal decisions;
    - modify governance status;
    - call an email provider directly;
    - interpret email delivery as approval.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        sender: ApprovalEmailSender,
        approval_recipient: str,
    ) -> None:
        if sender is None:
            raise ValueError("sender cannot be None.")

        if not approval_recipient.strip():
            raise ValueError("approval_recipient is required.")

        self._sender = sender
        self._recipient = approval_recipient.strip()

    def escalate(
        self,
        change: PrivacyChange,
        *,
        validation_passed: bool,
    ) -> ApprovalEmailRequest:
        """Create and send a governance approval request.

        A request can only be escalated after automated privacy
        validation has passed.

        Sending the email never constitutes DPO/GRC approval.
        """

        if change is None:
            raise ValueError("change cannot be None.")

        if not validation_passed:
            raise ValueError(
                "Cannot request approval while privacy "
                "validation is failing."
            )

        if not (
            change.dpo_required
            or change.grc_required
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Email escalation is only required for "
                "governance-routed changes."
            )

        roles: list[str] = []

        if change.dpo_required:
            roles.append("DPO")

        if change.grc_required:
            roles.append("GRC")

        subject = (
            "[EasyChatBot Privacy Governance] "
            f"Approval Required â€” {change.change_id}"
        )

        body = (
            "EASYCHATBOT Â· PRIVACY GOVERNANCE\n"
            "\n"
            "HUMAN GOVERNANCE REVIEW REQUIRED\n"
            "\n"
            "A privacy-impacting change has passed automated "
            "privacy validation and requires explicit human "
            "governance review before it may proceed.\n"
            "\n"
            "CHANGE\n"
            f"Change ID: {change.change_id}\n"
            f"Title: {change.title}\n"
            f"Description: {change.description}\n"
            f"Risk: {change.risk.value}\n"
            "\n"
            "GOVERNANCE\n"
            f"Required reviewers: {', '.join(roles)}\n"
            "Automated privacy validation: PASS\n"
            "Current governance status: PENDING_DPO_GRC\n"
            "\n"
            "DECISION REQUIRED\n"
            "Please review the controlled Privacy Change Registry "
            "and explicitly record the applicable governance decision.\n"
            "\n"
            "Possible decisions:\n"
            "- APPROVE\n"
            "- REJECT\n"
            "- REQUEST CHANGES\n"
            "\n"
            "IMPORTANT GOVERNANCE CONTROL\n"
            "This email is an escalation and approval request only.\n"
            "It does not itself constitute approval.\n"
            "Sending, receiving, or replying to this email does not "
            "constitute DPO or GRC approval.\n"
            "\n"
            "The change must remain pending until the required "
            "governance decision is explicitly recorded in the "
            "controlled governance system.\n"
            "\n"
            "Applicable GDPR references should be reviewed in the "
            "controlled privacy assessment for this change.\n"
            "\n"
            "EasyChatBot Privacy & Governance\n"
            "Automated validation provides decision support and "
            "control evidence. It does not replace DPO, GRC, or "
            "Legal judgement."
        )

        request = ApprovalEmailRequest(
            change_id=change.change_id,
            recipient=self._recipient,
            subject=subject,
            body=body,
            requires_dpo_approval=change.dpo_required,
            requires_grc_approval=change.grc_required,
        )

        self._sender.send(request)

        return request


__all__ = [
    "ApprovalEmailRequest",
    "ApprovalEmailSender",
    "PrivacyApprovalEmailService",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .privacy_governance_reminder_history import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_policy import (
    GovernanceReminderPolicy,
    build_decision_proposal,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_result import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult,
)


class GovernanceReminderStatePort(Protocol):
    def get_last_sent(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...

    def record(
        self,
        *,
        case_id: str,
        sent_at: datetime,
        recipient_role: str,
        email_reference: str | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class GovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    def send_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        proposal: Any,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    async def send_governance_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        recipient_role: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class InMemoryGovernanceReminderState(
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory
):
    """Process-local Beta reminder state."""

    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GovernanceReminderRunResult:
    """Aggregate result for the operational follow-up invocation."""

    scanned: int
    reminded: int
    case_ids_reminded: tuple[str, ...]


class PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort(Protocol):
    def all(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
        ...

    def get(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler:
    """Single governance reminder workflow with compatibility contracts.

    Supported application contract:

        run_once()
        reminder_state
        email_sender

    Supported cybersecurity contract:

        run()
        run_case()
        history
        email_service

    This class is not an APScheduler implementation. It is an application
    workflow invoked by the approved scheduler runtime.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        case_registry: Any,
        policy: GovernanceReminderPolicy,
        reminder_state: GovernanceReminderStatePort | None = None,
        email_sender: GovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
        history: PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory | None = None,
        email_service: PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_registry cannot be None."
            )

        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "policy cannot be None."
            )

        self.case_registry = case_registry
        self.policy = policy

        self.reminder_state = (
            reminder_state
            or history
            or InMemoryGovernanceReminderState()
        )

        self.email_sender = email_sender
        self.email_service = email_service

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6J operational follow-up API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run_once(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        """Run the synchronous operational follow-up contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        cases = tuple(
            self.case_registry.all()
        )

        reminded: list[str] = []

        for case in cases:
            if self._run_sync_case(
                case,
                now=current,
            ):
                reminded.append(
                    case.case_id
                )

        return GovernanceReminderRunResult(
            scanned=len(cases),
            reminded=len(reminded),
            case_ids_reminded=tuple(reminded),
        )

    def _run_sync_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return False

        if self.email_sender is None:
            return False

        proposal = build_decision_proposal(case)

        self.email_sender.send_reminder(
            case=case,
            proposal=proposal,
        )

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
        )

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Existing cybersecurity async API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult, ...]:
        """Evaluate all cases through the async email-service contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        results: list[
            PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult
        ] = []

        for case in self.case_registry.all():
            results.append(
                await self._evaluate_case(
                    case,
                    now=current,
                )
            )

        return tuple(results)

    async def run_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        """Evaluate one case through the async contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        case = self.case_registry.get(
            case_id
        )

        return await self._evaluate_case(
            case,
            now=current,
        )

    async def _evaluate_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        if not self.policy.is_eligible(case):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="case_not_pending_dpo_grc",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="reminder_interval_or_cooldown_not_reached",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        reason = (
            "pending_dpo_grc_reminder_due"
        )

        if self.email_service is None:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="not_configured",
            )

        try:
            response = (
                await self.email_service.send_governance_reminder(
                    case=case,
                    recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                    reason=reason,
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="EMAIL_FAILED",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="failed",
                error=str(exc),
            )

        email_status = "sent"
        reference: str | None = None
        case_url: str | None = None

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            email_status = str(
                response.get(
                    "status",
                    "sent",
                )
            )

            value = (
                response.get("reference")
                or response.get("message_id")
            )

            if value is not None:
                reference = str(value)

            url = response.get("case_url")

            if url is not None:
                case_url = str(url)

        elif response is not None:
            reference = str(response)

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            email_reference=reference,
        )

        return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
            status="REMINDER_SENT",
            case_id=case.case_id,
            governance_status=case.governance_status,
            due=True,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            reason=reason,
            generated_at=now,
            email_status=email_status,
            email_reference=reference,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_now(
        now: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if now.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "now must be timezone-aware."
            )


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "GovernanceReminderRunResult",
    "GovernanceReminderStatePort",
    "InMemoryGovernanceReminderState",
    "PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Protocol

from ..privacy_governance_case_url import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder,
)


class PrivacyGovernanceEmailSender(Protocol):
    """Adapter expected to deliver HTML email through the existing provider."""

    async def send(
        self,
        *,
        recipient: str,
        subject: str,
        body: str,
        html_body: str,
        attachments: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService:
    """Build and deliver a concise human-review reminder.

    This service is deliberately separate from the initial
    approval-required email.

    Responsibilities:
    - Build the governance reminder.
    - Build the controlled case URL.
    - Render HTML and plain-text representations.
    - Attach the governance logo inline.
    - Delegate delivery to the existing email provider.

    This service does NOT:
    - approve a case;
    - reject a case;
    - request changes;
    - modify governance state;
    - interpret email delivery as a governance decision.
    """

    sender: PrivacyGovernanceEmailSender
    recipient: str
    case_url_builder: PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder
    logo_path: Path

    def _render_html(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        case_url: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Render the concise HTML governance reminder."""

        change_id = (
            getattr(case, "change_id", None)
            or "N/A"
        )

        title = getattr(
            case,
            "title",
            "Privacy governance case",
        )

        risk = getattr(
            case,
            "risk",
            "N/A",
        )

        validation = getattr(
            case,
            "validation_status",
            "N/A",
        )

        case_id = getattr(
            case,
            "case_id",
            None,
        )

        if not case_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "case.case_id is required."
            )

        return f"""<!doctype html>

  <meta charset="utf-8">

    <div style="background:#ffffff;border:1px solid #e1e7f0;
    border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;">

      <div style="padding:28px 32px 18px;">
        <div style="font-size:12px;letter-spacing:2px;font-weight:700;
        color:#183b73;">
          EASYCHATBOT Â· PRIVACY GOVERNANCE
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style="padding:10px 32px 30px;">

        color:#183b73;margin-bottom:8px;">

        </div>

        color:#17233c;">
          Your review is still required
        </h1>

        <p style="margin:0 0 22px;font-size:16px;line-height:1.6;
        color:#52627a;">
          This privacy governance case is still waiting for human review.
        </p>

        <div style="background:#f7f9fc;border:1px solid #dfe6f0;
        border-radius:10px;padding:20px 22px;margin-bottom:24px;">

          <div style="display:inline-block;background:#eaf1fb;color:#183b73;
          border-radius:999px;padding:7px 12px;font-size:12px;
          font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px;">
            PENDING DPO/GRC
          </div>

          <div style="font-size:22px;font-weight:700;color:#17233c;
          margin-bottom:5px;">
            {case_id}
          </div>

          <div style="font-size:16px;color:#52627a;">
            {title}
          </div>

        color:#68778d;margin-bottom:10px;">

            <td style="padding:8px 0;color:#68778d;">
              Risk
            </td>

            color:#17233c;">
              {risk}
            </td>
          </tr>

          <tr>
            <td style="padding:8px 0;color:#68778d;">

            </td>

            </td>
          </tr>

            <td style="padding:8px 0;color:#68778d;">

        <div style="text-align:center;margin:6px 0 26px;">

          >
            REVIEW THIS CASE â†’
          </a>
        </div>

        <p style="margin:0;text-align:center;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;
        color:#68778d;">
          Open the EasyChatBot Privacy Governance Center to review the case
          and record the applicable governance decision.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div style="border-top:1px solid #e5e9f0;padding:24px 32px;
      background:#fbfcfe;">

        color:#52627a;margin-bottom:9px;">
          IMPORTANT GOVERNANCE CONTROL
        </div>

        <p style="margin:0 0 9px;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6;
        color:#68778d;">
          This email is a notification and escalation mechanism only.
          It does not constitute DPO approval, GRC approval, rejection,
          or legal advice.
        </p>

        <p style="margin:0;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6;color:#68778d;">
          The controlled governance system remains the authoritative record
          of the decision.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div style="text-align:center;padding:24px 24px 30px;">

        <img
          src="cid:easychatbot-governance-logo"
          alt="EasyChatBot"
          width="120"
          style="display:block;width:120px;height:auto;margin:0 auto 14px;
          border:0;"
        >

        <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#183b73;">
          EasyChatBot Privacy &amp; Governance
        </div>

        <div style="max-width:470px;margin:8px auto 0;font-size:11px;
        line-height:1.6;color:#8793a5;">
          Automated validation provides decision-support evidence.
          It does not replace DPO, GRC, Legal, or other authorised
          human judgement.
        </div>
      </div>

    </div>
  </div>
</body>
</html>"""

    def _render_text(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        case_url: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Render the plain-text fallback."""

        change_id = (
            getattr(case, "change_id", None)
            or "N/A"
        )

        case_id = getattr(
            case,
            "case_id",
            None,
        )

        if not case_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "case.case_id is required."
            )

        return (
            "EASYCHATBOT Â· PRIVACY GOVERNANCE\n\n"
            "REMINDER â€” YOUR REVIEW IS STILL REQUIRED\n\n"
            "This privacy governance case is still waiting "
            "for human review.\n\n"
            "PENDING DPO/GRC\n"
            f"{case_id}\n"
            f"{getattr(case, 'title', 'Privacy governance case')}\n\n"
            "CASE DETAILS\n"
            f"Change ID: {change_id}\n"
            f"Risk: {getattr(case, 'risk', 'N/A')}\n"
            f"Validation: "
            f"{getattr(case, 'validation_status', 'N/A')}\n"
            "Required reviewers: DPO + GRC\n\n"
            "REVIEW THIS CASE:\n"
            f"{case_url}\n\n"
            "IMPORTANT GOVERNANCE CONTROL\n"
            "This email is a notification and escalation mechanism only. "
            "It does not constitute DPO approval, GRC approval, rejection, "
            "or legal advice.\n\n"
            "The controlled governance system remains the authoritative "
            "record of the decision.\n\n"
            "EasyChatBot Privacy & Governance\n"
        )

    async def send_governance_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        recipient_role: str = "DPO/GRC",
        reason: str = "pending_case_reminder",
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Build and send one controlled governance reminder."""

        del recipient_role
        del reason

        if self.sender is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "sender cannot be None."
            )

        if not isinstance(self.recipient, str):
            raise ValueError(
                "recipient is required."
            )

        if not self.recipient.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "recipient is required."
            )

        if self.case_url_builder is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_url_builder cannot be None."
            )

        if self.logo_path is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "logo_path cannot be None."
            )

        if not self.logo_path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(
                "Governance email logo not found: "
                f"{self.logo_path}"
            )

        case_id = getattr(
            case,
            "case_id",
            None,
        )

        if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "case.case_id is required."
            )

        case_url = self.case_url_builder.build(
            case_id
        )

        subject = (
            "[EasyChatBot Privacy Governance] "
            f"Reminder â€” Decision Required for {case_id}"
        )

        html_body = self._render_html(
            case=case,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

        text_body = self._render_text(
            case=case,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

        response = await self.sender.send(
            recipient=self.recipient.strip(),
            subject=subject,
            body=text_body,
            html_body=html_body,
            attachments=[
                {
                    "path": str(self.logo_path),
                    "filename": "easychatbot_logo.png",
                    "content_id": "easychatbot-governance-logo",
                    "inline": True,
                }
            ],
        )

        status = "sent"
        reference: str | None = None

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            status = str(
                response.get(
                    "status",
                    "sent",
                )
            )

            value = (
                response.get("reference")
                or response.get("message_id")
            )

            if value is not None:
                reference = str(value)

        return {
            "status": status,
            "reference": reference,
            "case_url": case_url,
        }


__all__ = [
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService",
    "PrivacyGovernanceEmailSender",
]

======================================================================
4. PRIVACY GOVERNANCE REMINDER EMAIL SENDER
======================================================================

FILE:
backend\app\infrastructure\privacy\privacy_governance_reminder_email_sender.py

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from backend.app.application.privacy.email.privacy_governance_reminder_email_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceEmailSender,
)
from backend.cybersecurity.email.email_provider_manager import (
    EmailProviderManager,
)


class ResendGovernanceReminderEmailSender:
    """Infrastructure adapter for privacy governance reminder emails.

    Delivery path:

        PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService
            |
            v
        ResendGovernanceReminderEmailSender
            |
            v
        EmailProviderManager
            |
            v
        ResendEmailProvider
            |
            v
        Resend

    The adapter translates the richer HTML/text reminder contract into
    the existing provider-independent email infrastructure.

    Does NOT:
    - approve or reject governance cases;
    - modify governance state;
    - bypass the reminder scheduler;
    - access provider credentials directly;
    - make autonomous governance decisions.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        provider_manager: EmailProviderManager | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._provider_manager = (
            provider_manager
            if provider_manager is not None
            else EmailProviderManager()
        )

    async def send(
        self,
        *,
        recipient: str,
        subject: str,
        body: str,
        html_body: str,
        attachments: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        """Deliver a governance reminder through the existing email boundary."""

        if not recipient or not recipient.strip():
            raise ValueError("Email recipient is required.")

        if not subject or not subject.strip():
            raise ValueError("Email subject cannot be empty.")

        if not body or not body.strip():
            raise ValueError("Email body cannot be empty.")

        if not html_body or not html_body.strip():
            raise ValueError("HTML email body cannot be empty.")

        content: dict[str, Any] = {
            "recipient": recipient.strip(),
            "body": body,
            "html_body": html_body,
            "email_type": "privacy_governance_reminder",
        }

        if attachments:
            content["attachments"] = attachments

        return await self._provider_manager.send(
            subject,
            content,
        )


__all__ = [
    "ResendGovernanceReminderEmailSender",
]

======================================================================
5. RESEND EMAIL PROVIDER
======================================================================

FILE:
backend\cybersecurity\email\resend_email_provider.py

======================================================================
"""
Resend email provider.

Infrastructure adapter responsible only for email delivery.

The provider supports multiple controlled email contracts:

- Cybersecurity notifications:
  rendered by the existing cybersecurity HTML renderer.

- Privacy Governance reminders:
  the application service supplies the authoritative HTML and
  plain-text content. The provider must deliver that content
  without replacing it with the cybersecurity template.

The provider does not:
- make governance decisions;
- modify governance state;
- execute remediation;
- generate application decisions;
- access provider credentials outside configuration.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import resend

from backend.cybersecurity.email.email_configuration import (
    EMAIL_FROM,
    RESEND_API_KEY,
)


class ResendEmailProvider:
    """
    Provider adapter for Resend email delivery.

    Routing rule:

        privacy_governance_reminder
            -> supplied html_body

        all other email types
            -> existing cybersecurity renderer

    This preserves the existing Cybersecurity email while allowing
    application-owned Privacy Governance templates to pass through
    unchanged.
    """

    provider_id = "resend"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        api_key: str | None = None,
        sender: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.api_key = api_key or RESEND_API_KEY
        self.sender = sender or EMAIL_FROM

        if not self.api_key:
            raise ValueError(
                "RESEND_API_KEY is not configured."
            )

        if not self.sender:
            raise ValueError(
                "Email sender is not configured."
            )

        resend.api_key = self.api_key

    async def send(
        self,
        subject: str,
        content: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Send an email through Resend.

        Privacy Governance emails provide their own HTML template.
        Cybersecurity emails continue to use _build_html().
        """

        if not isinstance(subject, str) or not subject.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Email subject cannot be empty."
            )

        if not isinstance(content, dict):
            raise TypeError(
                "Email content must be a dictionary."
            )

        recipient = content.get("recipient")

        if not isinstance(recipient, str) or not recipient.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Email recipient is required."
            )

        recipient = recipient.strip()

        email_type = content.get(
            "email_type",
            "",
        )

        # ========================================================
        # HTML routing
        # ========================================================
        #
        # Privacy Governance owns its complete presentation.
        #
        # The reminder service already generated:
        #
        # - EasyChatBot Privacy Governance branding
        # - CASE-E2E-001
        # - PCR-E2E-001
        # - risk
        # - validation
        # - DPO/GRC reviewer information
        # - REVIEW THIS CASE button
        # - case URL
        # - governance disclaimer
        # - EasyChatBot logo CID
        #
        # Do NOT replace that HTML with the Cybersecurity renderer.
        #
        if email_type == "privacy_governance_reminder":
            html = self._get_supplied_html_body(content)
        else:
            # Existing Cybersecurity behaviour remains unchanged.
            html = self._build_html(content)

        # ========================================================
        # Plain-text body
        # ========================================================

        text_body = content.get("body")

        if text_body is not None:
            if not isinstance(text_body, str):
                raise TypeError(
                    "body must be a string when provided."
                )

            if not text_body.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    "body cannot be empty when provided."
                )

        # ========================================================
        # Resend payload
        # ========================================================

        params: dict[str, Any] = {
            "from": self.sender,
            "to": [recipient],
            "subject": subject,
            "html": html,
        }

        if isinstance(text_body, str) and text_body.strip():
            params["text"] = text_body

        attachments = content.get("attachments")

        if attachments is not None:
            params["attachments"] = self._build_attachments(
                attachments
            )

        response = await resend.Emails.send_async(
            params
        )

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            message_id = response.get("id")
        else:
            message_id = getattr(
                response,
                "id",
                None,
            )

        return {
            "provider": self.provider_id,
            "status": "accepted",
            "message_id": message_id,
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _get_supplied_html_body(
        content: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> str:
        """
        Return the application-owned Privacy Governance HTML.

        The provider deliberately does not generate or alter this
        HTML because the application service owns the presentation
        and governance message.
        """

        html_body = content.get("html_body")

        if not isinstance(html_body, str):
            raise TypeError(
                "Privacy Governance email requires "
                "html_body to be a string."
            )

        if not html_body.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Privacy Governance email html_body cannot be empty."
            )

        return html_body

    @staticmethod
    def _build_attachments(
        attachments: Any,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Convert application attachment metadata into Resend
        attachment payloads.

        Supports the Privacy Governance inline logo:

            cid:easychatbot-governance-logo
        """

        if not isinstance(attachments, list):
            raise TypeError(
                "attachments must be a list when provided."
            )

        normalized: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        for attachment in attachments:
            if not isinstance(attachment, dict):
                raise TypeError(
                    "Each email attachment must be a dictionary."
                )

            path_value = attachment.get("path")

            if not path_value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Email attachment path is required."
                )

            path = Path(str(path_value))

            if not path.exists():
                raise FileNotFoundError(
                    f"Email attachment not found: {path}"
                )

            if not path.is_file():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Email attachment is not a file: {path}"
                )

            filename = attachment.get("filename")

            resolved_filename = (
                str(filename).strip()
                if filename
                else path.name
            )

            if not resolved_filename:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Email attachment filename is required."
                )

            content_id = attachment.get(
                "content_id"
            )

            inline = bool(
                attachment.get(
                    "inline",
                    False,
                )
            )

            if inline and not content_id:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Inline email attachments require "
                    "content_id."
                )

            resend_attachment: dict[str, Any] = {
                "path": str(path),
                "filename": resolved_filename,
            }

            if content_id:
                resend_attachment["content_id"] = str(
                    content_id
                )

            normalized.append(
                resend_attachment
            )

        return normalized

    @staticmethod
    def _build_html(
        content: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> str:
        """
        Existing Cybersecurity HTML renderer.

        This remains the Cybersecurity fallback and is intentionally
        not used for Privacy Governance reminders.
        """

        posture = str(
            content.get(
                "posture",
                "Security notification",
            )
        )

        critical_count = content.get(
            "critical_count",
            0,
        )

        high_count = content.get(
            "high_count",
            0,
        )

        priority_signals = content.get(
            "priority_signals",
            [],
        )

        signal_rows = ""

        if isinstance(
            priority_signals,
            list,
        ):
            for signal in priority_signals:
                if not isinstance(
                    signal,
                    dict,
                ):
                    continue

                title = str(
                    signal.get(
                        "title",
                        "Security signal",
                    )
                )

                severity = str(
                    signal.get(
                        "severity",
                        "",
                    )
                ).upper()

                signal_rows += (
                    "<li>"
                    f"<strong>{title}</strong>"
                    f" — {severity}"
                    "</li>"
                )

        return f"""
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
    <meta charset="utf-8">
    <title>EasyChatBot Cybersecurity</title>
</head>
<body>
    <h2>EasyChatBot Cybersecurity Notification</h2>

    <p>
        <strong>Security posture:</strong>
        {posture}
    </p>

    <p>
        <strong>Critical findings:</strong>
        {critical_count}
    </p>

    <p>
        <strong>High findings:</strong>
        {high_count}
    </p>

    <h3>Priority Security Signals</h3>

    <ul>
        {signal_rows}
    </ul>

    <hr>

    <p>
        This notification is decision support only.
        No remediation action was executed by EasyChatBot.
    </p>

    <p>
        <strong>Environment:</strong> DEMO
    </p>
</body>
</html>
"""


__all__ = [
    "ResendEmailProvider",
]

======================================================================
6. EMAIL PROVIDER MANAGER
======================================================================

FILE:
backend\cybersecurity\email\email_provider_manager.py

======================================================================
"""
Email Provider Manager.

Controls provider selection, configuration, availability,
and controlled email delivery.

The manager is deliberately provider-independent.
Concrete providers such as Resend are injected into this
boundary rather than called directly by application services.

Does NOT:
- generate cybersecurity reports;
- schedule emails;
- analyse security;
- execute remediation;
- expose provider credentials.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from backend.cybersecurity.email.resend_email_provider import (
    ResendEmailProvider,
)


class EmailProviderManager:
    """
    Provider abstraction for controlled email delivery.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        provider: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:

        self.provider = (
            provider
            if provider is not None
            else self._build_default_provider()
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_default_provider() -> Any | None:
        """
        Build the configured default email provider.

        Resend is currently the approved hackathon provider.

        If Resend is not configured, return None so the
        application can report provider_not_configured
        rather than failing during application import.
        """

        try:
            return ResendEmailProvider()

        except ValueError:
            return None

    def status(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return non-sensitive provider status.

        This method never exposes:
        - API keys;
        - secrets;
        - credentials.

        Status is determined from the provider already
        configured in the manager. No external provider
        request is made.
        """

        if self.provider is None:

            return {
                "provider": None,
                "configured": False,
                "available": False,
                "status": "not_configured",
            }

        provider_id = getattr(
            self.provider,
            "provider_id",
            self.provider.__class__.__name__,
        )

        return {
            "provider": provider_id,
            "configured": True,
            "available": True,
            "status": "ready",
        }

    async def send(
        self,
        subject: str,
        content: dict,
    ) -> dict:

        if self.provider is None:

            return {
                "status": "provider_not_configured",
            }

        return await self.provider.send(
            subject,
            content,
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from urllib.parse import quote


class PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder:
    """Build stable application URLs for human governance review."""

    def __init__(self, *, app_base_url: str) -> None:
        base = app_base_url.strip().rstrip("/")
        if not base:
            raise ValueError("app_base_url is required.")
        self._base_url = base

    def build(self, case_id: str) -> str:
        if not isinstance(case_id, str) or not case_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("case_id is required.")
        return (
            f"{self._base_url}/privacy-governance/"
            f"{quote(case_id.strip(), safe='')}"
        )


__all__ = ["PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder"]

8. PRIVACY GOVERNANCE COMPOSITION

FILE:
backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_composition.py

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from .privacy_approval_email_service import PrivacyApprovalEmailService
from .privacy_approval_registry import PrivacyApprovalRegistry
from .privacy_change_registry import PrivacyChangeRegistry
from .privacy_governance_case_registry import PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry
from .privacy_governance_case_service import PrivacyGovernanceCaseService
from .privacy_governance_dashboard_service import PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService
from .privacy_governance_decision_service import PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService
from .privacy_governance_flow_service import PrivacyGovernanceFlowService
from .privacy_governance_gate import PrivacyGovernanceGate
from .privacy_validation_service import PrivacyValidationService


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Single composition root for the privacy governance capability.

    Beta 1 uses in-memory registries. Application services receive those same
    instances so case creation, decisions, dashboard reads, and API reads all
    observe one coherent runtime state.

    Repository-backed implementations can replace the registries later without
    changing the API contract or frontend.
    """

    change_registry: PrivacyChangeRegistry
    case_registry: PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry
    approval_registry: PrivacyApprovalRegistry
    decision_service: PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService
    governance_gate: PrivacyGovernanceGate
    case_service: PrivacyGovernanceCaseService
    flow_service: PrivacyGovernanceFlowService
    dashboard_service: PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService


def create_privacy_governance_composition(
    *,
    validation_service: PrivacyValidationService | None = None,
    approval_email_service: PrivacyApprovalEmailService | None = None,
) -> PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Create an isolated composition, primarily for tests and explicit DI."""

    change_registry = PrivacyChangeRegistry()
    case_registry = PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistry()
    approval_registry = PrivacyApprovalRegistry()

    decision_service = PrivacyGovernanceDecisionService(
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        change_registry=change_registry,
        case_registry=case_registry,
    )

    governance_gate = PrivacyGovernanceGate(
        registry=change_registry,
        validation_service=validation_service,
        approval_email_service=approval_email_service,
    )

    case_service = PrivacyGovernanceCaseService(
        case_registry=case_registry,
        change_registry=change_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        decision_service=decision_service,
    )

    flow_service = PrivacyGovernanceFlowService(
        change_registry=change_registry,
        governance_gate=governance_gate,
        case_service=case_service,
    )

    dashboard_service = PrivacyGovernanceDashboardService(
        case_registry=case_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
    )

    return PrivacyGovernanceComposition(
        change_registry=change_registry,
        case_registry=case_registry,
        approval_registry=approval_registry,
        decision_service=decision_service,
        governance_gate=governance_gate,
        case_service=case_service,
        flow_service=flow_service,
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
    )


# Runtime composition boundary.
# The FastAPI adapter and application integrations must use this instance.
_runtime_composition: PrivacyGovernanceComposition | None = None


def get_privacy_governance_composition() -> PrivacyGovernanceComposition:
    """Return the process-wide Beta governance composition.

    This is the shared runtime instance used by the Operations Center API and
    by application integrations that submit privacy governance events.

    A future repository-backed implementation can keep this boundary while
    replacing the underlying registry adapters.
    """

    global _runtime_composition

    if _runtime_composition is None:
        _runtime_composition = create_privacy_governance_composition()

    return _runtime_composition


def reset_privacy_governance_composition_for_tests() -> None:
    """Reset the runtime composition.

    Intended for test isolation only. Production code must not reset runtime
    governance state.
    """

    global _runtime_composition
    _runtime_composition = None

9. PRIVACY GOVERNANCE GATE
======================================================================

FILE:
backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_gate.py

======================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace
from typing import Any

from .privacy_approval_email_service import (
    PrivacyApprovalEmailService,
)
from .privacy_change import (
    PrivacyChange,
    PrivacyChangeStatus,
)
from .privacy_change_registry import (
    PrivacyChangeRegistry,
)
from .privacy_validation_service import (
    PrivacyValidationResult,
    PrivacyValidationService,
)


class PrivacyGovernanceGate:
    """Privacy governance decision gate.

    Responsibilities:
    - consume deterministic privacy validation results;
    - block changes with validation failures;
    - route privacy-impacting changes to DPO/GRC;
    - trigger an approval escalation only when validation passes;
    - never interpret email delivery as approval.

    Does NOT:
    - make legal decisions;
    - grant DPO/GRC approval;
    - execute production changes;
    - schedule recurring approval emails.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        registry: PrivacyChangeRegistry,
        validation_service: PrivacyValidationService | None = None,
        approval_email_service: PrivacyApprovalEmailService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "registry cannot be None."
            )

        self._registry = registry
        self._validation = validation_service
        self._approval_email = approval_email_service

    def route(
        self,
        change: PrivacyChange,
        *,
        automated_validation_passed: bool | None = None,
        dpo_approved: bool = False,
        grc_approved: bool = False,
    ) -> PrivacyChange:
        """Route a change through the governance decision.

        This synchronous method preserves the existing Beta contract.

        If a validation service is configured, it is the authoritative
        source for automated privacy validation.
        """

        if change is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "change cannot be None."
            )

        validation_passed = self._resolve_validation(
            automated_validation_passed
        )

        if not validation_passed:
            updated = replace(
                change,
                status=PrivacyChangeStatus.BLOCKED,
            )

        elif change.dpo_required or change.grc_required:
            if not (
                dpo_approved
                and grc_approved
            ):
                updated = replace(
                    change,
                    status=(
                        PrivacyChangeStatus.PENDING_DPO_GRC
                    ),
                )
            else:
                updated = replace(
                    change,
                    status=(
                        PrivacyChangeStatus.APPROVED
                    ),
                )

        else:
            updated = replace(
                change,
                status=(
                    PrivacyChangeStatus.APPROVED
                ),
            )

        self._save(updated)

        return updated

    async def route_and_escalate(
        self,
        change: PrivacyChange,
    ) -> PrivacyChange:
        """Run automated validation and escalate when governance requires it.

        Email is sent ONLY when:

        1. PrivacyValidationService returns PASS;
        2. the change requires DPO/GRC review;
        3. the change is not already approved.

        Email delivery never changes the governance status to APPROVED.
        """

        if change is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "change cannot be None."
            )

        if self._validation is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PrivacyValidationService is required "
                "for automatic governance routing."
            )

        if self._approval_email is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "PrivacyApprovalEmailService is required "
                "for automatic governance escalation."
            )

        validation = self._validation.validate()

        if validation.status != "PASS":
            updated = replace(
                change,
                status=PrivacyChangeStatus.BLOCKED,
            )

            self._save(updated)
            return updated

        if not (
            change.dpo_required
            or change.grc_required
        ):
            updated = replace(
                change,
                status=PrivacyChangeStatus.APPROVED,
            )

            self._save(updated)
            return updated

        updated = replace(
            change,
            status=(
                PrivacyChangeStatus.PENDING_DPO_GRC
            ),
        )

        self._save(updated)

        self._approval_email.escalate(
    change=updated,
    validation_passed=True,
)
        return updated

    def can_proceed(
        self,
        change_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        change = self._registry.get(
            change_id
        )

        return bool(
            change
            and change.status
            == PrivacyChangeStatus.APPROVED
        )

    def _resolve_validation(
        self,
        explicit_result: bool | None,
    ) -> bool:
        if self._validation is not None:
            result = self._validation.validate()

            return (
                result.status == "PASS"
            )

        if explicit_result is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "automated_validation_passed is required "
                "when no PrivacyValidationService is configured."
            )

        return explicit_result

    def _save(
        self,
        change: PrivacyChange,
    ) -> None:
        existing = self._registry.get(
            change.change_id
        )

        if existing is None:
            self._registry.register(
                change
            )
        else:
            self._registry.update(
                change
            )

======================================================================
10. PRIVACY CHANGE DOMAIN MODEL
======================================================================

FILE:
backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_change.py

======================================================================
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any


class PrivacyChangeStatus(str, Enum):
    DRAFT = "draft"
    ASSESSED = "assessed"
    PENDING_DPO_GRC = "pending_dpo_grc"
    APPROVED = "approved"
    BLOCKED = "blocked"


class PrivacyChangeRisk(str, Enum):
    LOW = "low"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    HIGH = "high"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PrivacyChange:
    """A controlled record for a potentially privacy-impacting change."""

    change_id: str
    title: str
    description: str
    affected_processing_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    affected_data_map_ids: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    data_categories_changed: bool = False
    purpose_changed: bool = False
    recipients_changed: bool = False
    retention_changed: bool = False
    processor_changed: bool = False
    transfer_changed: bool = False
    automated_decision_changed: bool = False
    dpia_screening_required: bool = False
    risk: PrivacyChangeRisk = PrivacyChangeRisk.LOW
    status: PrivacyChangeStatus = PrivacyChangeStatus.DRAFT
    dpo_required: bool = False
    grc_required: bool = False
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    created_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    )

from backend.app.application.privacy.privacy_approval_email_service import (
    PrivacyApprovalEmailService,
)
from backend.app.application.privacy.privacy_change import (
    PrivacyChange,
    PrivacyChangeRisk,
)


class FakeSender:
    def __init__(self):
        self.requests = []

    def send(self, request):
        self.requests.append(request)


def make_change():
    return PrivacyChange(
        change_id="PCR-EMAIL-001",
        title="Add new learner analytics",
        description="Introduce a new privacy-impacting analytics flow.",
        risk=PrivacyChangeRisk.MEDIUM,
        dpo_required=True,
        grc_required=True,
    )


def test_email_escalation_requires_human_approval():
    sender = FakeSender()
    service = PrivacyApprovalEmailService(
        sender=sender,
        approval_recipient="configured-reviewer@example.test",
    )

    request = service.escalate(
        make_change(),
        validation_passed=True,
    )

    assert request.recipient == "configured-reviewer@example.test"
    assert request.change_id == "PCR-EMAIL-001"
    assert request.requires_dpo_approval is True
    assert request.requires_grc_approval is True
    assert len(sender.requests) == 1
    assert "does not itself constitute approval" in request.body


def test_email_escalation_refuses_failed_validation():
    sender = FakeSender()
    service = PrivacyApprovalEmailService(
        sender=sender,
        approval_recipient="configured-reviewer@example.test",
    )

    try:
        service.escalate(make_change(), validation_passed=False)
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "validation" in str(exc).lower()
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Escalation was allowed despite failed validation")

    assert sender.requests == []

12. TESTS — REMINDER EMAIL

FILE:
tests\cybersecurity\test_privacy_governance_reminder_email_service.py

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from backend.app.application.privacy.email.privacy_governance_reminder_email_service import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService,
)
from backend.app.application.privacy.privacy_governance_case_url import (
    PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Case:
    case_id: str = "CASE-E2E-001"
    change_id: str = "PCR-E2E-001"
    title: str = "New learner data processing"
    risk: str = "MEDIUM"
    validation_status: str = "PASS"
    governance_status: str = "PENDING_DPO_GRC"


class FakeSender:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = []

    async def send(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return {
            "status": "sent",
            "message_id": "MSG-001",
        }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_reminder_is_concise_and_contains_case_link_and_logo(tmp_path: Path):
    logo = tmp_path / "easychatbot_logo.png"
    logo.write_bytes(b"logo")

    sender = FakeSender()
    service = PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService(
        sender=sender,
        recipient="dpo@example.test",
        case_url_builder=PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder(
            app_base_url="http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        ),
        logo_path=logo,
    )

    result = await service.send_governance_reminder(case=Case())

    assert result["status"] == "sent"
    assert result["reference"] == "MSG-001"

    call = sender.calls[0]
    assert call["subject"] == (
        "[EasyChatBot Privacy Governance] "
        "Reminder â€” Decision Required for CASE-E2E-001"
    )
    assert "REVIEW THIS CASE" in call["html_body"]
    assert (
        "http://127.0.0.1:5173/privacy-governance/CASE-E2E-001"
        in call["html_body"]
    )
    assert "easychatbot-governance-logo" in call["html_body"]
    assert call["attachments"][0]["content_id"] == (
        "easychatbot-governance-logo"
    )
    assert call["attachments"][0]["inline"] is True

    # Reminder must remain shorter in scope than the initial approval email:
    assert "Possible decisions:" not in call["html_body"]
    assert "ACTION REQUIRED" not in call["html_body"]

"""
Tests for the Resend cybersecurity email provider.
"""

import pytest

from backend.cybersecurity.email.resend_email_provider import (
    ResendEmailProvider,
)


def test_resend_provider_requires_api_key_when_configuration_missing(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Provider must reject missing API credentials when no
    configured environment value exists.
    """

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "backend.cybersecurity.email.resend_email_provider.RESEND_API_KEY",
        "",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ResendEmailProvider(
            api_key=None,
            sender="EasyChatBot <test@example.com>",
        )


def test_resend_provider_requires_sender_when_configuration_missing(
    monkeypatch,
):
    """
    Provider must reject missing sender configuration when no
    configured sender exists.
    """

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        "backend.cybersecurity.email.resend_email_provider.EMAIL_FROM",
        "",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        ResendEmailProvider(
            api_key="test-api-key",
            sender=None,
        )


def test_resend_provider_has_expected_identity():
    """
    Provider exposes its stable provider identifier.
    """

    provider = ResendEmailProvider(
        api_key="test-api-key",
        sender="EasyChatBot <test@example.com>",
    )

    assert provider.provider_id == "resend"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_resend_provider_rejects_empty_subject():
    """
    Provider must reject an empty email subject.
    """

    provider = ResendEmailProvider(
        api_key="test-api-key",
        sender="EasyChatBot <test@example.com>",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await provider.send(
            "",
            {
                "recipient": "test@example.com",
            },
        )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_resend_provider_requires_recipient():
    """
    Provider must reject email payloads without a recipient.
    """

    provider = ResendEmailProvider(
        api_key="test-api-key",
        sender="EasyChatBot <test@example.com>",
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        await provider.send(
            "Test",
            {},
        )


def test_resend_provider_builds_security_html():
    """
    Provider must render the important cybersecurity
    intelligence fields into the email HTML.
    """

    html = ResendEmailProvider._build_html(
        {
            "posture": "HIGH ATTENTION REQUIRED",
            "critical_count": 0,
            "high_count": 1,
            "medium_count": 2,
            "low_count": 1,
            "signal_count": 4,
            "open_signal_count": 4,
            "priority_signals": [
                {
                    "title": "Prompt injection detected",
                    "severity": "high",
                }
            ],
            "decision_note": (
                "Review the highest-priority signals."
            ),
            "execution_id": "test-execution",
            "data_mode": "DEMO",
        }
    )

    assert "HIGH ATTENTION REQUIRED" in html

    assert (
        "Prompt injection detected"
        in html
    )

    assert "DEMO" in html

    assert (
        "decision support only"
        in html
    )

    assert (
        "No remediation action was executed"
        in html
    )

    EMAIL_RECIPIENT,
)


BACKEND_CASE_URL = (
    "http://127.0.0.1:8000"
    "/api/privacy/governance/cases/CASE-E2E-001"
)

FRONTEND_BASE_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:5173"

LOGO_PATH = (
    Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
    / "backend"
    / "app"
    / "application"
    / "privacy"
    / "email"
    / "assets"
    / "easychatbot_logo.png"
)

SUCCESS_STATUSES = {
    "accepted",
    "sent",
    "success",
}


def load_case() -> SimpleNamespace:
    """Load the authoritative case from the running FastAPI application."""

    request = Request(
        BACKEND_CASE_URL,
        method="GET",
    )

    with urlopen(request, timeout=10) as response:
        if response.status != 200:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"Case API returned HTTP {response.status}."
            )

        payload = json.loads(
            response.read().decode("utf-8")
        )

    return SimpleNamespace(
        case_id=payload["case_id"],
        change_id=payload.get("change_id"),
        title=payload.get(
            "title",
            "Privacy governance case",
        ),
        risk=payload.get(
            "risk",
            "N/A",
        ),
        validation_status=payload.get(
            "validation_status",
            "N/A",
        ),
        governance_status=payload.get(
            "governance_status",
            "PENDING_DPO_GRC",
        ),
    )


async def main() -> None:
    print("=" * 70)
    print("EASYCHATBOT PRIVACY GOVERNANCE REAL EMAIL TEST")
    print("=" * 70)

    if not EMAIL_RECIPIENT.strip():
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No email recipient is configured."
        )

    if not LOGO_PATH.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Governance logo not found: {LOGO_PATH}"
        )

    print()
    print("Recipient:")
    print(EMAIL_RECIPIENT)

    print()
    print("Loading authoritative case from:")
    print(BACKEND_CASE_URL)

    case = load_case()

    print()
    print("Case:")
    print(case.case_id)

    print("Change:")
    print(case.change_id)

    print("Governance status:")
    print(case.governance_status)

    case_url_builder = PrivacyGovernanceCaseURLBuilder(
        app_base_url=FRONTEND_BASE_URL,
    )

    expected_case_url = case_url_builder.build(
        case.case_id
    )

    print()
    print("Generated case URL:")
    print(expected_case_url)

    sender = ResendGovernanceReminderEmailSender()

    service = PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService(
        sender=sender,
        recipient=EMAIL_RECIPIENT,
        case_url_builder=case_url_builder,
        logo_path=LOGO_PATH,
    )

    print()
    print("Sending real reminder email through:")
    print("PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailService")
    print("        ->")
    print("ResendGovernanceReminderEmailSender")
    print("        ->")
    print("EmailProviderManager")
    print("        ->")
    print("Resend")

    result = await service.send_governance_reminder(
        case=case,
    )

    print()
    print("=" * 70)
    print("EMAIL DELIVERY RESULT")
    print("=" * 70)

    print(json.dumps(result, indent=2))

    provider_status = result.get("status")

    if provider_status not in SUCCESS_STATUSES:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Reminder email was not accepted by the email provider: "
            f"{result}"
        )

    returned_url = result.get("case_url")

    if returned_url != expected_case_url:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Generated case URL mismatch: "
            f"expected={expected_case_url!r}, "
            f"returned={returned_url!r}"
        )

    print()
    print("SUCCESS")
    print("-" * 70)
    print("Case ID:", case.case_id)
    print("Email status:", provider_status)
    print("Email reference:", result.get("reference"))
    print("Case URL:", returned_url)
    print()
    print("Provider accepted the email for delivery.")
    print()
    print("Open the email and click:")
    print("REVIEW THIS CASE")
    print()
    print("Expected destination:")
    print(expected_case_url)
    print("=" * 70)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())
